It's not necessarily true that they must all have valid perceptions which are identical. If they did, then the three beings looking at the glass of water would have to think of the water as a place to live, in the way that a creature living in water would. The three beings as well would have to see the water in the same way that microscopic organisms living in the water, little beings imperceptible to normal visual consciousness, see it with their own visual consciousness. Then too the visual consciousness of microscopic organisms living in the depths of the ocean would have to be a valid perception towards the entire extent of the sea. And certain kinds of near-gods too would have to see weapons as glasses of water, and on and on; the problems raised would be many."

    "english": "Generally speaking, each of the three objects mentioned--the pus and the other two--are things of the type that block other objects from entering into the space which they themselves occupy. It is no contradiction though to say that, in this situation where the beings are looking this way, they are not objects such that they block other things from entering into the space they occupy. This is true for the following reason. A central mountain of the world which is square in shape, and a central mountain of the world which is round in shape, and the like, are objects such that they block other things from entering the space they occupy. Nevertheless, it is possible for both these things to occupy the space taken up by a single central mountain of the world. A red-hot ball of steel is something that's hot, but consider what happens when a person touches it after he has used the mantra of steel on his hand. The sensation that he feels is not a sensation of heat; on the contrary, it is a sensation of something not heat. [This concludes the section of the text entitled \"The Stream.\"] 128 The Asian Classics Institute Course V: How Karma Works Reading Seven: Black and White Deeds, the \"Path of Action,\" and the Root and Branch Non-Virtues"

===== reconciled/310/report.md =====
C05:310 — Codex independent reconciliation; PROVISIONAL machine alignment.
Counts: 40 spans; 1 nulls; 26 total d5; 25 non-null d5 pairs; 8 d7 members; 0 retained errata.
Both originals preserve the same first/second conduct, birth and experience occurrences; the full source and both complete frozen records were checked independently.
The complete opening conditional is d2. Retain all eight strict members under four established compounds; English bzang/ngan atoms become members of the attested bzang ngan parent.
Retain khyod kyi/your at d3. thal is a lexical consequence predicate at d5, narrowed to have to; supplied would and be remain outside.
lus is the sole null: no separate body/bodily exponent. Negative/exclusion scope in ma gtogs, mi myong and med par remains omitted, with original quotes and reasons.

===== tibetan/311/report.md =====
C05:311 — Tibetan-first — Codex provisional machine proposal.
First challenge: omitted med par thal is a shared negative-existence consequence expanded twice and as You must be. No auxiliary-rich d5 pair or false null is proposed; the two agree occurrences and realm genitives are distinct.
Omissions: the supplied misperception explanation, shared med par thal and de'i phyir recast. C15:456 matches three text fields; C16:848 adds a heading. Neither establishes independent publication.
Source/evidence: ../evidence/spine-full-original-rows.json; complete original master/corpus/physical bytes and exact references in ../evidence/evidence-index.json (SHA256 19bc09149520202ab678a34526ce5109302f090dd3030aa0c90e937adc02f757). Historical origin 51d73c889a3f175b90e4c466f3dc4b1f7af3bca5 through306; no future acceptance baseline is claimed.
Local source/English ranges and occurrence-specific reasoning: source-specific-rationale.json and resolved-ranges.json. Physical ACIP/English extents and original-byte hashes: physical-source-proof.json.

===== reconciled/312/report.md =====
C05:312 — Codex independent reconciliation; PROVISIONAL machine alignment.
Counts: 57 spans; 0 nulls; 41 total d5; 41 non-null d5 pairs; 6 d7 members; 0 retained errata.
Retain all three mig shes parents with two strict members each, distinguishing normal vision, the glass organisms' own vision, and ocean organisms' vision.
gnas/place and the two residence predicates select living at418 and721; the repeated living at537 stays unwrapped. Retain complete blos/think, supported locally and by the shared cognitive relation in original C05:250.
Retain nang/depths plus na/in in the ocean clause, and the cup's whole nang du/in phrase. Tighten both ocean stems and the later gnas pa off attached genitives.
Opening tshad/valid perceptions differs from ocean tshad/entire extent. The complete latter phrase is HGM-attested and suits the whole sea's measure; the initial narrower draft was preserved and replaced after source review.
Keep the narrow have to necessity occurrences and the ocean's constitutive 'gro/be. First tshad mar 'gro and mi bzod are distributed or unresolved and remain omitted, not null.
Only the clear near-god lexical words remain; no full mtshon cha phor gang/as glasses of water relation is certified. C15:457/C16:849/C18:1133 are digital repeats, not independent-publication proof.
All 101 original proposed spans have explicit dispositions in original-dispositions.json; 91 retain exact correspondence and 10 are explicitly recast/tightened. Original IDs, depths, words, occurrence ranges and parent links remain preserved.
